- EC 3.6.1.3 = NTP phosphohydrolase (ATP → ADP + phosphate)
- EC 3.6.1.5 = NTP diphosphohydrolase
- EC 3.1.3.1 = alkaline phosphatase

For Rho-family GTPases, the EC number assigned is typically EC 3.6.5.2, which corresponds to &quot;small monomeric GTPase&quot; activity - the hydrolysis of GTP to GDP and phosphate.

EC 3.6.5.2 is specifically for small monomeric GTPases including those in the Rho family. This EC number describes the reaction: GTP + H2O = GDP + phosphate.

The question specifically mentions &quot;Rho-family classification&quot; which strongly points to EC 3.6.5.2.

- Contains P-loop (GxxxxGKT)
- Contains conserved regions typical of small GTPases
- The question explicitly states &quot;Rho-family classification&quot;

The answer is C. EC 3.6.5.2
